He said out loud at about half past twelve, in two hundred and twenty-three words, that he had been calling that building a shelter since yesterday morning and that he was wrong, and he read out the three things a sounding is to be called safe against, which a clerk had entered on the seventeenth of the sixth month, and said he was not saying anything about a bell and was saying that the third of the three is not true and a charter does not make it true. **HIS RIGHT HAND HAD NOT CLOSED PROPERLY SINCE ABOUT HALF PAST TWO YESTERDAY AFTERNOON AND THE CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS HAD TO OPEN IT WITH HER FINGERS ONE AT A TIME, AND HE CARRIED TWO BUCKETS OF WATER IN FROM THE TOP OF THAT LANE IN TWO TRIPS, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT THERE ARE ABOUT NINE INCHES OF ROPE LEFT INSIDE A BEAM IN A FRAME OF OAK FOUR MILES OUT PAST THE LOAMS, AND THAT A BELL DOES NOT NEED A PERSON AND THAT A PERSON STANDING NEXT TO A BELL IS NOT DECIDING WHAT IT RINGS, AND THAT NOBODY IN THAT LANE HAS ASKED WHETHER IT SHOULD BE PULLED.**

## Chapter 0323 — The Sixth of the Same Shape

**The twelfth of the eighth month, eleven days past. The board at 231, the train at 547, the unentered days at 261, two hundred and twenty-two from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and fourteen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-one days, the table for the thirty-seventh morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and thirteenth days, the rival record seventy-two days, the hundred and thirty-fourth day since anybody wrote on a board. A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS ENTERED SIX SHAPES OF A THING IN THIS DISTRICT IN ONE HAND AND NOT AS A PARAGRAPH, THE SIXTH OF THEM BEING THE COLUMN RULED THE DAY BEFORE INSIDE THE BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS WITH THE WORD NOT ASKED OVER IT AND NOTHING UNDER IT, AND ENTERED THAT THE SIX ARE THE SAME SHAPE OF THING, THAT NONE OF THE SIX IS A PERSON, THAT THE SIX ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER, THAT A SIXTH OF A SHAPE OF THING IS NOT A SIXTH OF THE FIVE PROTECTED THINGS AND IS NOT A SEVENTH OF THE SIX INSTRUMENTS, AND THAT A COLUMN OF THE SAME SHAPE IS NOT THE SAME COLUMN AND THE THIRD COLUMN OF A COVENANT IN A BOOK A STRANGER MAY WALK UP TO IS A DIFFERENT COLUMN AND IS STILL RULED AND STILL EMPTY.** The man of about thirty-four who digs loam said, in a hundred and thirty-eight words, that he is not saying the sixth is worse than the other five and that the word over his is NOT ASKED and the word over the one on that gatepost is a job nobody in this district has, and that he would put them in the same drawer, and that he has got a bad arm and is not going to make it into an argument about handwriting. **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST SAID THE OPPOSITE OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD IN A HUNDRED AND SEVENTY-TWO WORDS, THAT HE THINKS THAT COLUMN IS WORTH MORE BECAUSE IT IS THE ONLY ONE THAT HAS NOTHING TO DO WITH HIM, AND THEN SAID THAT IS NOT MORE HONEST, IT IS LESS, AND THAT A DOCUMENT THAT CANNOT BE WRONG ABOUT ANYBODY CANNOT BE RIGHT ABOUT ANYBODY, AND THAT IT IS A DRAWER AND HE IS NOT GOING TO HAVE IT IN A DRAWER.** The man of about twenty-three who copies for nothing copied the eight lines out between about half past two and about five. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing went up the bank at about half past four, stood at the step of two stones, put his hand on the wood beside the sheet of print the man of about sixty-four laid there on the ninth of the eighth month, took it away, and did not knock.

## Chapter 0324 — Two Lines

**The thirteenth of the eighth month, twelve days past. The board at 232, the train at 548, the unentered days at 262, two hundred and twenty-three from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and fifteen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-two days, the table for the thirty-eighth morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and fourteenth days, the rival record seventy-three days, the hundred and thirty-fifth day since anybody wrote on a board. TWO TERMS OF THE PUBLIC MARKET OF THIS DISTRICT WERE WRITTEN IN A ROOM A STRANGER MAY WALK UP TO, ONE OF THEM ABOUT WATER, SHELTER AND MEDICINE AND ONE OF THEM ABOUT A SOUND, AND THE SECOND OF THEM IS THAT NO PERSON IN THIS DISTRICT IS TO BE ENTERED IN ANY RECORD OF IT BY HAVING HEARD A SOUND AND THAT NOBODY IS TO BE ASKED WHETHER THEY HEARD ONE.** He got both of them down in about twenty minutes and then could not hold the pen up in his right hand and wrote the second in his left. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing read them out loud in the ordinary voice to about nine people without being handed the sheet and asked, in a hundred and nine words, which one of them it is for, and said he was asking for the finger and not for the name. **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST ANSWERED THAT IT IS FOR NOBODY AND THAT THERE IS NO FINGER AND THAT A DISTRICT IS NOT A BODY AND CANNOT BE NAMED ON A SHEET OF ITS OWN, AND THAT A TERM NOBODY CAN ENFORCE AND EVERYBODY CAN READ IS NOT THE SAME THING AS A TERM NOBODY CAN ENFORCE AND NOBODY CAN READ, IN TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY-EIGHT WORDS. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A TERM WITH NOBODY'S NAME AGAINST IT BINDS NOBODY, THAT THIS DISTRICT FOUND THAT OUT ON THE PAGE AND ENTERED IT, AND THAT THE TERM STANDS ANYWAY, AND THAT THE STANDING OF IT IS NOT A FINDING AND IS NOT A SUCCESS.** A clerk entered that the two pieces of paper in the book four hundred yards from that gate are not corrected and are not taken out, and that a list of who came into a building is not a list of who heard a bell, and that the second of the two lines is the answer to that difference and the answer is not in the book. He carried the sheet out and laid it on the table with four legs and weighted the corner with a stone about the size of a fist.

## Chapter 0325 — Eleven Words

**The fourteenth of the eighth month, thirteen days past. The board at 233, the train at 549, the unentered days at 263, two hundred and twenty-four from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and sixteen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-three days, the table for the thirty-ninth morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and fifteenth days, the rival record seventy-four days, the hundred and thirty-sixth day since anybody wrote on a board. THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SIX ASKED HER WHETHER SHE WOULD PUT HER NAME UNDER THE WORD NOT ASKED IN THE ROLL, AND SHE REFUSED IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HER OWN, IN A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-EIGHT WORDS: IF I PUT MY NAME UNDER THAT WORD, THEN I AM IN A COLUMN, AND THE COLUMN IS IN A BOOK, AND IN NINE YEARS SOMEBODY WHO HAS NEVER MET ME WILL READ THAT I WAS ASKED AND I SAID NO, AND A PIECE OF PAPER IS THE EASY WAY AND I AM NOT GOING TO TAKE IT BECAUSE A MAN IN THIS YARD HAS BEEN CAREFUL. A clerk entered that the two refusals given in that yard on the fifth and sixth of the eighth month and this one are three refusals on three days of three different things asked of one person by three different men, and that three is a figure about three refusals and is not a figure about her. SHE WAS NOT IN THAT YARD AGAIN THAT AFTERNOON AND THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST DID NOT GO AFTER HER AND SENT NOBODY.** At about four in the afternoon she went into a room about nine feet by nine at the top of that lane, past the third house from the scale, with nothing in her hands, and nothing came out in her hands, and nobody wrote anything down, and she chose the man of about thirty-four who mends fencing out of three people in this district she had reason to say it to. **SHE SAID ELEVEN WORDS, ONCE. THEY ARE NOT ON THE PAGE AND ARE NOT IN ANY LEDGER AND NOT IN ANY FOOTER. THEY CARRY NO NAME, NO PLACE, NO FIGURE AND NO BODY, THEY ARE A CONDITION AND NOT A PROMISE, THEY DO NOT SAY WHAT SHE WILL DO, SHE DID NOT SAY THEM A SECOND TIME TO ANYBODY, AND NOBODY ASKED HER TO.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud in that yard at about six, in a hundred and thirteen words, that she said eleven words to him in a room and that he is not going to say what they were and nobody in that yard is going to make him, and that it was a condition and not a promise and was not about her. **A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS ENTERED THAT ELEVEN IS A FIGURE ABOUT ELEVEN WORDS AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A PERSON, AND THAT A CLAUSE IS A DOCUMENT AND THAT ELEVEN WORDS IN A ROOM ARE NOT A DOCUMENT AND DID NOT BECOME ONE — THE WORD *CLAUSE* IS USED ONCE IN THIS VOLUME AND IT IS THERE.** The man the figure of twenty-one years is against was at the bank from about ten until about six and could not write at all on the fourteenth.

## Chapter 0326 — Two Figures of the Same Length

**The fifteenth of the eighth month, fourteen days past. The board at 234, the train at 550, the unentered days at 264, two hundred and twenty-five from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and seventeen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-four days, the table for the fortieth morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and sixteenth days, the rival record seventy-five days, the hundred and thirty-seventh day since anybody wrote on a board. THE VOLUME'S REVERSAL, AND IT IS NOT ABOUT THE BELL.** The man the figure of twenty-one years is against went into the building with two doors at about eleven with the sheet of five lines of print that came off a stake in that gatepost on the seventh of the eighth month, and had the two figures in front of him at about half past eleven with a clerk of nineteen years standing in the room, and a clerk entered the whole of the day as one line and not as a paragraph: a man of about thirty-four who keeps a road counted the edge of a sound out from a bell four miles out past the loams on the twenty-sixth and twenty-seventh of the seventh month and brought back eleven miles, the sheet nailed to a gatepost says eleven miles, the two are the same length, and he did not put them together and did not write one of them next to the other and stood there for about forty minutes and then put the five lines of print back inside his coat and said out loud that he was not going to do it. He had the whole of it by about one: that if the sound of that bell heard the ground the sheet is about then every name in the bell's record is the name of somebody who did not come to a door, and that the only man in this district who was in the sound of it was standing about nine feet off the end of that trestle table, and that the man had not been asked one question about that night and he was not going to be the first one. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX SAID IN A HUNDRED AND SIXTEEN WORDS THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO PUT TWO FIGURES TOGETHER AND THAT NOBODY PUT ONE OF THEM THERE AND THAT HE HAS NOT GOT AN ARGUMENT AND IS NOT GOING TO HAVE ONE TODAY. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A CLERK WHO COULD HAVE MADE A FINDING OUT OF TWO FIGURES AND DID NOT IS NOT A CLERK WHO HAS FOUND NOTHING, AND ENTERED THAT AGAINST THE CLERK AND NOT AGAINST THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX.** The man the figure of twenty-one years is against said out loud at about four, in a hundred and ninety-five words, that there is a list bigger than four hundred and eleven and that he cannot recommend it and cannot refuse it and is not the one holding it, and that a number made by knocking, a number made by walking and a number made by listening are three numbers and are not added together, and that he is not going to make the finding and is not going to stop anybody making it, and that he is going to write down that he had it in his hand at half past eleven this morning and put it down. Nobody in that yard asked the man of about sixty-four anything about the night of the twenty-fourth of the sixth month, and the man the figure of twenty-one years is against stood about nine feet off him for about forty minutes at about one o'clock and did not ask him anything.

## Chapter 0327 — A Shelter Cannot Be Rehearsed

**The sixteenth of the eighth month, fifteen days past. The board at 235, the train at 551, the unentered days at 265, two hundred and twenty-six from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and eighteen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-five days, the table for the forty-first morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and seventeenth days, the rival record seventy-six days, the hundred and thirty-eighth day since anybody wrote on a board. THE SHEET OF PRINT IS STILL ON THE TOP STEP OF TWO STONES OUTSIDE THAT DOOR, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT WHERE A SHEET IS IS NOT AN ANSWER TO ANYTHING, AND THAT A SHEET ON A STEP IS A STATE AND NOT A PLACE, AND THAT NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS BEEN ASKED WHETHER ANYBODY WILL READ IT.** THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST SAID THE WORD *PRACTISE* OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD AT ABOUT ELEVEN IN A HUNDRED AND EIGHT WORDS, BECAUSE HE HAD BEEN AWAKE SINCE ABOUT FIVE AND BECAUSE HE HAD CONVENED NINE SETTLEMENTS AND HAD NOT GOT ONE OF THEM TO A SECOND MORNING. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM ANSWERED IN A HUNDRED AND FORTY-TWO WORDS: YOU CANNOT PRACTISE A THING WITH PEOPLE WHO HAVE NOT BEEN ASKED, BECAUSE A PRACTICE OF A THING IS A THING DONE BY PEOPLE WHO AGREED TO DO IT, AND THERE IS NOBODY IN THIS YARD WHO HAS AGREED TO DO IT AND NOBODY YOU ARE ALLOWED TO ASK, AND IF YOU ASK THEM THEN IT IS NOT A PRACTICE, IT IS AN INSTRUCTION.** A clerk entered that a practice of a thing is a thing done by people who agreed to do it. **NOBODY WENT TO THE BANK. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO KEEPS A SCALE WAS AT THE BANK FROM BEFORE EIGHT UNTIL ABOUT FIVE WITH ABOUT NINE SACKS WEIGHED AND THE BLANKET FOLDED OVER HER ARM, WHICH SHE DOES NOT DO, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD WENT DOWN THAT LANE TO ASK HER ANYTHING, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT SHE IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT THE ROLL AND IS NOT A REFUSAL OF IT AND THAT NEITHER OF THOSE IS THE OTHER. A CHILD OF ABOUT EIGHT WAS AT THE FOOT OF THAT GATEPOST FROM ABOUT ELEVEN UNTIL ABOUT ONE WITH A STICK ABOUT FOUR FEET LONG AND WAS ASKED NOTHING BY ANYBODY IN THAT YARD, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A CHILD IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A ROLL AND IS NOT A REFUSAL OF ANYTHING. THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT CAME DOWN THE BANK AT ABOUT FIVE WITH TWO BUCKETS AND SAID, TO NOBODY AND NOT COUNTED, THAT THERE IS ONE OF THE ELEVEN SHE CARRIES TO THAT SHE HAS NOT ASKED A QUESTION ABOUT IN NINETEEN YEARS AND THAT SHE WAS NOT GOING TO ASK IT, AND THE NINTH OF THE ELEVEN WAS NOT ASKED.** He could not get his right hand to hold the near corner of that sheet of print on the bench on the sixteenth and the clerk of nineteen years lifted it and set it on the bench.

## Chapter 0328 — The Proof Is Unavailable

**The seventeenth of the eighth month, sixteen days past. The board at 236, the train at 552, the unentered days at 266, two hundred and twenty-seven from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and nineteen days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-six days, the table for the forty-second morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and eighteenth days, the rival record seventy-seven days, the hundred and thirty-ninth day since anybody wrote on a board.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing asked one question in about eighty-four words, in front of about nine people, which was how anybody in this basin finds out, on some morning in six months, whether that sheet did the thing or did not do the thing. The man the figure of twenty-one years is against went round the back of that building and looked at the ditch for about nine minutes and stood about four feet off the end of that trestle table for about twenty minutes without saying anything at anybody, and then said, in a hundred and eighty-five words: **THERE IS NO WAY, I HAVE GONE THROUGH IT IN MY HEAD TWICE THIS MORNING AND THERE IS NO WAY, THE ONLY PEOPLE WHO CAN TELL YOU WHETHER THAT ROLL WORKS ARE THE PEOPLE IT IS FOR, AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS AGREED TO BE TOLD ANYTHING, AND I AM NOT ALLOWED TO ASK, AND THE PROOF IS NOT UNAVAILABLE TODAY, IT IS NOT GOING TO BECOME AVAILABLE, THAT IS NOT A GAP THAT CLOSES, THAT IS WHAT IT IS.** A clerk entered that a document built for persons who have not been asked has not been tested against anybody, and that it is being signed anyway, and that this is what a convening is, and that a convening is not the same thing as a safe thing, and that the two words are not the same word. **HE WENT IN AT ABOUT HALF PAST THREE AND RULED A LINE ABOUT TWO INCHES LONG AT THE FOOT OF THAT SHEET AND PUT HIS NAME ON IT, IN CHALK, WITH HIS LEFT HAND, BECAUSE HIS RIGHT HAND WOULD NOT CLOSE AND THERE IS NO INK IN THAT BUILDING AND NEVER HAS BEEN, AND THE MARK CAME OUT SMALL AND PALE AND ABOUT A SIXTEENTH OF AN INCH OFF THE LINE AT ONE END, AND HE LOOKED AT IT FOR A WHILE AND DID NOT RUB IT OUT. NO NAME IS PRINTED IN THE CHAPTER.** A clerk entered that the line carries one name and that the name is his own on his own line, and that a name on a line in a room a stranger may walk up to is a name a stranger can be shown, and that one name in a room four hundred yards from a gate is not one name added to the four hundred and eleven and does not make four hundred and twelve, and that a line with a name on it is not a line with a word over it and nothing under it and is not a seventh of the six shapes. He stood on the step of two stones at about five with the five lines of print in his coat and did not pick it up and did not knock.

**The eighteenth of the eighth month, seventeen days past. The board at 237, the train at 553, the unentered days at 267, two hundred and twenty-eight from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty days back, the pool four months back and a hundred and forty-seven days, the table for the forty-third morning, the chair sat in once, the six households a hundred and nineteenth days, the rival record seventy-eight days, the hundred and fortieth day since anybody wrote on a board. A woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat asked, in a hundred and seventy-one words, what the roll says about the lane, and said that she is not asking to be on it and that the last line of the first sheet says a person leaves it when they go and there is nothing in eight lines and two terms about where you go to, and that if she is the one who has to stand in that lane in the morning she would like a line about it and there is not one and he did not put one there on purpose. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT THE ROUTES OF THE PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN ANY BOOK ARE NOT WRITTEN DOWN ANYWHERE IN THIS DISTRICT AND ARE NOT GIVEN UP AND ARE NOT IN THAT ROLL, AND THAT THE ROLL SAYS NOTHING ABOUT THE WAY OUT OF IT, AND THAT A STRANGER MAY WALK UP TO THAT ROLL AND READ THAT THERE IS NO WAY OUT OF IT WRITTEN ON IT, AND THAT THAT IS WHAT THE ROLL IS FOR.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said in a hundred and sixty-five words that nobody said one word about the last line, and that a thing that says a person leaves it when they go is the absence of a rule, and that it is not a cage, and that he does not know what it is and is not going to stand in a yard and give it a name so the rest of them feel better about it. **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST WAS AT THE END OF THAT TABLE FOR BOTH READINGS AND DID NOT READ EITHER OF THEM AND DID NOT SPEAK.**

THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS STILL FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN.** About half past ten he took the roll and the two terms, which had lain on the table with four legs from about ten yesterday morning, and laid them flat side by side and put the stone on the corner of both, and he used his left hand because his right one has not closed properly since the tenth of this month, and a clerk of nineteen years stood about nine feet off him and did not offer to do it and did not say anything about it. **THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE BOOKS IN THE ROOM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE CAME DOWN THAT BANK AT ABOUT HALF PAST FOUR ON HER OWN TWO FEET AND NOBODY WENT WITH HER AND NOBODY MET HER AT THE BOTTOM OF IT, AND CAME ACROSS THE YARD AND STOPPED ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE END OF THAT TABLE, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT SHE WAS NOT ASKED TO COME OUT OF THAT ROOM AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED.** She said one thing out loud in a hundred and forty-nine words: that she has read the sheet that was on her own step and has not read the eight lines and is not going to say why, and that she said in this basin that there is going to be one line in that book because of something she did, and that she has not written it, and that there is not going to be one in either of those two sheets, and that she did not write either of them and was not asked to and is not going to be. A clerk entered that he did not ask her one question that afternoon, did not ask anybody else to ask her one, did not go after her and sent nobody, and that he asked for nothing. He said one thing at about five, in two hundred and seventeen words: that she came out of that room; that there is a building with two doors at the end of that lane and it is not a shelter and nobody in this district has said it is; that there are two terms on a table outside it and one of them binds nobody and both of them can be read by anybody; that there are eleven words in a room at the top of that lane that are not a document; that he has got a proof he cannot get and three people he has not asked and a document he signed anyway; and that he is going to sit down on that step for about nine minutes and is not going to knock. The roll and the two terms were read out once more at about half past six and read back from the top and got nothing wrong. **THE THIRD COLUMN OF A COVENANT IN A BOOK A STRANGER MAY WALK UP TO IS STILL RULED AND STILL EMPTY, AND THE NINTH CHARTER'S LINE AND THE NINTH PLACE ON A PAGE OUT OF A CAVE ARE STILL RULED AND STILL EMPTY, AND THE THREE OF THEM ARE NOT FILLED BY A COLUMN RULED IN A BUILDING AT THE END OF A LANE, AND THE NINTH OF THE NINE PRINTED NIGHTS WAS A HUNDRED AND TWENTY-ONE DAYS BACK AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT CAN SAY WHETHER THAT NIGHT HAPPENED, AND A PLANK DOES NOT KEEP NIGHTS.**

## Chapter 0331 — Nine Inches of Rope

**The twentieth of the eighth month, nineteen days past the removal day. The board at 239, the train at 555 days, the unentered days at 269, two hundred and thirty from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-two days back, the pool of refusals with no reason a clerk of a house has given four months back and a hundred and forty-nine days, the table against that wall for the forty-fifth morning, the chair sat in once on the tenth of this month and not a ladder, the six households a hundred and twenty-first days, the rival record eighty days, a hundred and forty-second day since anybody wrote on a board. THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS STILL FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN.** **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING WENT FOUR MILES OUT PAST THE LOAMS AND TIED A KNOT IN ABOUT NINE INCHES OF ROPE WITH THE FINGERS OF HIS RIGHT HAND, BECAUSE HIS LEFT HAND HAS NOT CLOSED SINCE THE ELEVENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AND HE DID NOT PULL IT, AND NOBODY DECIDED THAT IT SHOULD BE PULLED.** The frame of oak is about eleven feet high with two uprights about four feet apart and a beam across the top, the bell in it is about two feet across the mouth and cracked from the rim up about a third of the way on the side that faces the loams, the clapper is in it, and about nine inches of rope has been hanging inside that beam since the night of the twenty-fourth of the sixth month at a height a man standing on the ground cannot reach. He got up it off the low brace at about the height of his hip and up the upright and along the upright, and the grain of that oak has gone soft at the top in nine years, and he was careful with his right elbow and did not trust his left for anything. **HE PUT THE END OF THE ROPE INTO THE FINGERS OF HIS RIGHT HAND, TOOK A TURN ROUND HIS THUMB, DREW IT OUT A HAND'S BREADTH, MADE A KNOT IN IT WITH THREE FINGERS AND A THUMB AND THE HEEL OF HIS PALM, AND PUT THE LOOP OF THAT KNOT OVER THE INSIDE OF HIS RIGHT WRIST WHERE THE PULSE IS.** A clerk of nineteen years entered that a knot in a rope is a knot and is not a job and is not a decision, and that **THE REASON FOR THAT KNOT IS IN THE BINDING AND NOT IN ANYBODY'S HEAD**, and that a man who tied one has not been asked to tie it and has not been asked to leave it, and that a bell does not need a person and a person standing on a frame is not standing in the bell's place. **The man the figure of twenty-one years is against said out loud at about seven, in two hundred and fourteen words, that he has a hand that will not close and has not slept in six nights, and that if anything is done in that lane at two in the morning it will be done by a person who was asked to do it or it will be done by nobody, and that he is not asking anybody this evening and is not going to ask anybody tomorrow. A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE HAS NOT ASKED ONE PERSON IN THAT YARD A QUESTION ABOUT THAT LANE AND HAS NOT ASKED ANYBODY ELSE TO ASK ONE.** She entered that the third of the three things a clerk wrote down on the seventeenth of the sixth month is a community providing a shelter and a way to take the record off, and that a building with two doors and a bench and a stool and two buckets of water is not a shelter and has not been called one by anybody in this district, and that a man of about thirty-eight who cuts reeds said so at a table on the eleventh of this month in about four seconds, and that the third of the three is not true and is not made true by a table with four legs outside a door.

**The twenty-first of the eighth month, twenty days past. NOTHING CAME OF THE DAY UNTIL ABOUT NINE IN THE EVENING.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing asked the man of about thirty-four who keeps a road one question at about four in the afternoon in about nine feet of lane, **and the question was whether he would go up that lane in the small hours and stand outside that building in the dark for about a quarter of an hour, AND NO SOUND HAD BEEN SAID OUT LOUD TO ANYBODY IN THAT YARD AND THE QUESTION DID NOT NAME ONE.** The man who keeps a road said no in about four seconds with a reason of his own — that he has already been out once, over ground he had never walked, that it took him two days and brought back eleven miles, and that he is not a person anybody in this district should want holding a lantern in the dark tonight. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT A REFUSAL WITH A REASON IS NOT ONE OF THE REFUSALS WITH NO REASON A CLERK OF A HOUSE HAS GIVEN, AND THAT THE EIGHT DID NOT MOVE THAT AFTERNOON, AND THAT A MAN OR A WOMAN WHO KEEPS A HOUSE IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE.** The man of about thirty-four who digs loam came up the lane at about five and said out loud, in two hundred and forty-six words, that nobody has asked him to stand in that lane tonight, and he wants that entered, because in nine weeks somebody will write that people stood in that lane and it will read as though they had been asked, and that if that bell goes at two then he will hear it from a bank four hundred yards off and he has not got a hook or a line or a lamp and has not asked anybody for one. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX SAID OUT LOUD AT ABOUT FIVE IN THE AFTERNOON, AND IT WAS NOT COUNTED, THAT HE IS NOT AFRAID OF THE NIGHT AND THAT HE IS AFRAID OF THE MORNING, AND THAT HE HAD SAID IT ONCE BEFORE IN THE FIFTEENTH YEAR AND HAD NOT SAID IT SINCE.** About nine people went up that lane at about nine o'clock. The man the figure of twenty-one years is against went up the bank at about half past eight, did not go into that building, went back down and out through that gate, and was not in this district again until about two in the morning. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing went out past the loams at about one in the morning and got up the frame and put the loop of the knot over the inside of his right wrist, and a clerk entered that a person standing on a frame is not deciding what the frame rings, and that **IT HAS NOT BEEN PULLED BETWEEN THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND THIS ONE.** At about two in the morning it went, for about five minutes, and then it went on past five minutes. **ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE WERE IN IT IN ABOUT TWO HUNDRED YARDS OF LANE AND IN ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF YARD, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT EVERY ONE OF THEM IS IN THE RECORD OF THE SECOND NIGHT AND CANNOT BE TAKEN OFF IT, AND THAT NOBODY ASKED THEM. NOBODY IN THAT SOUND WAS ASKED WHETHER THEY HEARD IT, AND NO HAND WENT UP AND NO NAME WAS GIVEN.**

## Chapter 0333 — Eleven Minutes After

**The twenty-second of the eighth month, twenty-one days past, and it came in with about four minutes of that sound still in it. IT WENT AT ABOUT THE FOURTH MINUTE OF IT AND EVERY MINUTE AFTER THAT HAS BEEN ON THE OTHER SIDE OF IT.** A clerk entered that a man who carries a message is not a keeper of it and does not become one and is not redeemed and is not a failure, and that the standing of a man in a yard at about twenty minutes past two in the morning is a standing and is not a post. **The man of fifty-six came out of a field at about a quarter past two and was in the last of it, and said out loud at about three, in two hundred and forty-eight words, that he said this in the fifteenth year out loud in a field, and said it again at five that afternoon, and is saying it a third time tonight, and that he is not afraid of the night and is afraid of the day after the morning, and that he is not a clerk, and that nobody is to put it in a column or put his name over it. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A SHAPE NOTICED A SECOND TIME IS NOT A FINDING AND A THIRD TIME IS A HABIT, AND THAT HE HAS NOW HEARD THAT BELL THREE TIMES, BEEN IN A FIELD TWICE AND BEEN TOLD NOTHING THREE TIMES.** **HE CAME OUT OF THE GATE AT ABOUT TEN PAST FOUR IN THE MORNING INTO A YARD WITH ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE STANDING IN IT WHO HAD BEEN STANDING IN IT SINCE ABOUT TWO IN THE MORNING, AND HE ASKED NOBODY WHAT HAD HAPPENED, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A SOUND WHICH GOES ELEVEN MILES DID NOT WAKE A MAN FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OFF IT, AND THAT THE ELEVEN MILES IS A FIGURE ABOUT A BELL AND THE FOUR HUNDRED YARDS IS A FIGURE ABOUT A MAN AND THE TWO ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER, AND THAT NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS A WAY OF DRAWING A LINE. HE STOOD AT THE END OF THAT TRESTLE TABLE FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES BEFORE HE COULD LOOK AT ANYTHING ON IT, BECAUSE THE YARD WAS TOO BRIGHT AFTER THE DARK.** A child of about eight was in the sound of it and was not asked anything by anybody in that yard, and a clerk entered that a child is not a figure about the second night and is not a refusal of anything.

## Chapter 0334 — The Column With a Word Over It

**The twenty-third of the eighth month, twenty-two days past, and the reading was set for eleven and it was in daylight. The board at 242, the train at 558, the unentered days at 272, two hundred and thirty-three from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-five days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-two days, the table for the forty-eighth morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-third days, the rival record eighty-three days, a hundred and forty-fifth day since anybody wrote on a board. THE POWER CHANGE, SPENT IN THE ACT OF NOT READING.** **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING READ OUT, FROM THE TOP, IN THE ORDINARY VOICE, STANDING ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE END OF THAT TRESTLE TABLE, THE ACCOUNT OF THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FIRST AND A LIST OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE WHO WERE IN THE SOUND OF THAT BELL, CALLED BY THE NAME EACH OF THEM IS CALLED IN THIS DISTRICT, AND THE LIST IS NOT PRINTED HERE. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES A CART READ THE WHOLE OF IT BACK FROM THE TOP AND GOT NOTHING WRONG, AND NEITHER READING WAS COUNTED.** A clerk entered that a thing read out in daylight in a yard a stranger can walk into and read back from the top is a thing that has been introduced and not a thing that has been proved, and that a list of about nineteen names read out in a yard is a list of about nineteen people who were in a sound and is not a list of about nineteen people who came into a building. **The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud, in two hundred and twenty-three words, that every one of those names went into that list because a person was standing in the open in the dark and not because anybody asked them anything, and that a name in that list cannot be taken off it by the person it belongs to, and that there is a line on a table outside that building ten days old that says no person in this district is to be entered in any record of it by having heard a sound and that nobody is to be asked whether they heard one, and that THERE IS NOBODY'S NAME AGAINST THAT LINE, THAT IS WHY IT IS NOT WORTH THE PAPER, AND IT IS ALSO THE ONLY THING IN THAT YARD THAT IS STANDING BETWEEN THOSE NINETEEN NAMES AND A STRANGER WHO WALKS IN THERE TOMORROW. I AM NOT GOING TO TELL YOU THAT IT SAVES US. I AM GOING TO TELL YOU IT IS THE ONLY THING THERE IS.** **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST STOOD ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE END OF THAT TABLE FROM ABOUT HALF PAST TEN UNTIL ABOUT TWENTY PAST TWELVE AND DID NOT READ EITHER PART OF IT, AND A CLERK ENTERED THE REASONS: A MARK MAY NOT CARRY A JOB, A PERSON STANDING NEXT TO A THING IS NOT STANDING IN ITS PLACE, HE WAS NOT IN THE SOUND OF IT BECAUSE HE WAS ASLEEP IN A BED FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OFF IT, AND A DOCUMENT IS READ OUT BY SOMEBODY WHO WAS IN THE THING.** He said out loud at about one, in two hundred and sixty-three words, that he is not going to read that, that he wrote eight lines in a building on the tenth of this month and signed them in chalk and a man read them out on the eighteenth and he said nothing and was wrong about that then and is wrong about it today for a different reason, and that there is a column ruled in a book four hundred yards from that gate with a word over it and nothing under it for the name of whoever read a thing out loud, that it has been ruled a long time, that his name is not going in it and he is not going to ask the woman who keeps that book to put it in, and that the reader is the mender. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT THE COLUMN IS A WORD OVER A RULED LINE WITH NOTHING UNDER IT AND IS THE SEVENTH OF THAT SHAPE IN THIS DISTRICT, IS NONE OF THE SIX, IS NOT THE SAME COLUMN AS ANY OF THE SIX, IS NOT ADDED TO THEM, IS NOT FILLED, AND THAT THE ONE NAME IN CHALK AT THE FOOT OF THE ROLL IS A LINE WITH A NAME ON IT AND IS NOT A SEVENTH OF ANYTHING.** What he lost is the reading, and it is not a limb and it is not his life and he is alive and about nine people in that yard spoke to him before eleven and after twelve. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING THIS MORNING AND DID NOT ASK ANYBODY ELSE TO ASK FOR ANYTHING FOR HIM, AND THAT THAT IS ENTERED AND IS NOT A FINDING AND IS NOT A REFUSAL AND IS NOT A GOING-AWAY.**

## Chapter 0335 — The One He Chose

**The twenty-fourth of the eighth month, twenty-three days past. The board at 243, the train at 559, the unentered days at 273, two hundred and thirty-four from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-six days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-three days, the table for the forty-ninth morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-fourth days, the rival record eighty-four days, a hundred and forty-sixth day since anybody wrote on a board. THE CHOOSING OF HER IS A REAL ACT AND IS IN A SCENE.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud at about ten, in two hundred and two words, that he is going to say it once in that yard and not in a lane, that the man at the foot of that wall has a name in his head that he has carried for eighteen years and it is the name of somebody in the four hundred and eleven, and that he is not going to ask him for it, and that anybody who wants to know what a name is worth to a body four hundred miles off holding a count of answerers can stand at about ten feet off that wall tonight and will get not one word more of it than the next person. **THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST SPENT THE WHOLE OF THAT DAY CHOOSING A PERSON, AND A CLERK ENTERED THE WHOLE OF THE DAY AS ONE LINE AND NOT AS A PARAGRAPH: ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF LANE AND ONE BANK AND ONE GATEPOST AND ONE ROOM WERE IN IT, AND FOUR PEOPLE WERE IN IT, AND ONE OF THEM IS A WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WHO CARRIES A SECOND BUCKET TO A HOUSE IN THE GROUND IN THE SHADOW OF A BUILDING ELEVEN MILES OFF AND IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THIS DISTRICT WHO HAS DONE THAT AND HAS NEVER PUT ONE QUESTION TO THAT HOUSE IN NINETEEN YEARS.** He did not go down the lane to the woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale, and a clerk entered that she is not a figure about a name and is not a refusal of one, and that her three words are rate and even and stock and that none of the three has been said again in this district and that none of them may be explained by her. He said two sentences to the woman of about thirty-four who keeps a goat at the foot of that gatepost and neither was a question. **HE WENT UP THE BANK AT ABOUT THREE, STOOD ON THE STEP OF TWO STONES WHERE A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR HAD LAID A SHEET OF PRINT FLAT ON THE NINTH OF THIS MONTH, DID NOT PICK IT UP, DID NOT KNOCK, AND CAME BACK DOWN AT ABOUT HALF PAST THREE, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN STANDING ON A STEP IS NOT HOLDING A SHEET AND IS NOT HOLDING A DOOR AND IS NOT HOLDING ANYTHING, AND THAT THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT KNOCKING SAYS NOT ASKED.** He said out loud at about half past four to the woman of fifty-eight at the foot of that bank, in two hundred and forty-one words, **THAT HE CHOSE HER, BECAUSE SHE IS THE ONLY ONE WHO HAS CARRIED A SECOND BUCKET TO A HOUSE IN THE GROUND AND HAS NEVER ASKED THAT HOUSE A QUESTION IN NINETEEN YEARS; THAT HE DID NOT CHOOSE THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS A SCALE BECAUSE HE HAS GOT NOTHING TO PUT IN HER HANDS AND SHE HAS FOUR HOUSES STANDING EMPTY AND NINE SACKS A DAY; THAT HE DID NOT CHOOSE THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS A GOAT BECAUSE SHE ASKED THE RIGHT QUESTION ABOUT THAT LANE ON THE EIGHTEENTH OF THIS MONTH AND IF HE PUTS A NAME IN HER HANDS SHE WILL DO SOMETHING WITH IT AND HE CANNOT ASK HER TO DO NOTHING WITH IT; AND THAT HE IS NOT ASKING HER WHAT SHE THINKS AND IS ASKING HER TO COME TO A ROOM WITH A SHUT DOOR IN IT AT HALF PAST FOUR TOMORROW.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing agreed to leave his room for about twenty minutes and said that he would rather not be in it either, and went down that lane and stood about nine feet off the end of that table for about twenty minutes and did not go up the bank.

## Chapter 0336 — Said Once, in the Ordinary Voice

**The twenty-fifth of the eighth month, twenty-four days past. The board at 244, the train at 560, the unentered days at 274, two hundred and thirty-five from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-seven days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-four days, the table for the fiftieth morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-fifth days, the rival record eighty-five days, a hundred and forty-seventh day since anybody wrote on a board. NOTHING HAPPENED ON THIS DAY UNTIL ABOUT HALF PAST FOUR IN THE AFTERNOON.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing asked him one question in that yard at about ten, in the ordinary voice, in front of about nine people, and **IT WAS NOT COUNTED**, which was what he was going to do with a name he was going to hear that afternoon, and why he was asking it in a yard in daylight instead of in that room. **HE DID NOT ANSWER HIM, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO HAS NOT ANSWERED A QUESTION IN A YARD HAS NOT REFUSED TO ANSWER IT AND CANNOT BE COUNTED EITHER WAY, AND THAT A QUESTION ASKED IN A YARD IS A QUESTION A PERSON CAN REFUSE.** The room is about nine feet by nine at the top of that lane, past the third house from the scale, with a stool by the wall and a window about a foot and a half square and a board nailed across the outside of it, and **THERE IS NO PAPER IN THAT ROOM AND NO INK IN THAT ROOM AND THERE NEVER HAS BEEN**, and the man the figure of twenty-one years is against and a man of about sixty-four were in it from about half past three and a woman of fifty-eight came into it at about half past four and stayed about twenty minutes. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT NOTHING WENT INTO THAT ROOM IN ANYBODY'S HANDS AND NOTHING CAME OUT OF IT IN ANYBODY'S HANDS AND THAT NOBODY WROTE ANYTHING DOWN IN IT, AND THAT A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS WAS FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY IN A YARD AND WAS NOT TOLD ABOUT IT UNTIL ABOUT SIX AND DID NOT GO UP THAT LANE, AND THAT A NAME WAS SAID ONCE IN THAT ROOM AND A CLERK MAY ENTER THAT A NAME WAS SAID AND NOT WHAT IT WAS.** The man of about sixty-four said one thing out loud at about ten past four before he said the other thing, in two hundred words: that he has carried it for eighteen years on his own two feet and never put it on anything and told nobody, and that there is one more in that night behind the twenty-fourth of the sixth and he is not going to say tonight whether that one is a name or a hand, and that he is going to say this one because the man has been asking him a question about it every day for a fortnight by standing nine feet off him and not asking it, and that he is going to say it to her and not to him, because she has been standing at the edge of that ground for nineteen years without putting a single question into it. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO HAS CARRIED SOMETHING FOR EIGHTEEN YEARS AND SAID IT OUT LOUD ONCE IN A ROOM HAS BEEN DELIVERED AND IS NOT A FAILURE AND IS NOT A TRAGIC FIGURE AND IS NOT REDEEMED, AND THAT A MAN WHOSE TRADE IS OVER IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT ANYBODY.** Then he said the name. It was said once, in the ordinary voice, in a room with a shut door in it, **AND IT IS NOT ON THIS PAGE. A CLERK ENTERED THAT THE NAME IS ONE OF THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN AND THAT THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN IS A COUNT OF THE PEOPLE WHO ANSWERED A DOOR, AND THAT A NAME SAID OUT LOAD IS A CONSENT THE PERSON IT BELONGS TO DID NOT GIVE, AND THAT BINDING CANNOT CREATE CONSENT AND NEITHER CAN A CERTIFICATE AND NEITHER CAN A BELL, AND THAT THE PERSON IT BELONGS TO HAS NOT BEEN ASKED AND HAS NOT REFUSED AND CANNOT BE COUNTED EITHER WAY, AND THAT NO PLACE IS NAMED WITH IT AND NO BODY IS NAMED WITH IT AND IT IS NOT ENTERED IN EITHER OF THE TWO BOOKS.** The woman of fifty-eight did not say it back and wrote it on nothing and asked him nothing about it. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing came up that lane at about five and found his room empty and stood in the doorway of it for about nine minutes, and said out loud at about six, in a hundred and seventy-two words, that he lent that room for twenty minutes and did not go back up that lane and has not asked him what was said in there and is not going to, and that a name is a different thing from eleven words and he does not know what the difference is worth and neither does anybody else in this basin, and that he is not going to be the man who says in this yard that he knows a name.

## Chapter 0337 — A Name in One Person's Head Is Not Safe

**The twenty-sixth of the eighth month, twenty-five days past. The board at 245, the train at 561, the unentered days at 275, two hundred and thirty-six from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-eight days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-five days, the table for the fifty-first morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-sixth days, the rival record eighty-six days, a hundred and forty-eighth day since anybody wrote on a board. SHE CAME DOWN THE BANK AT ABOUT FIVE WITH TWO BUCKETS, THE WAY SHE DOES, AND BEFORE SHE ASKED ANYBODY FOR ANYTHING AND BEFORE ANYBODY HAD ASKED HER FOR ANYTHING SHE SAID IT OUT LOUD IN THE ORDINARY VOICE IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE. THE TWO WORDS ARE NOT ON THIS PAGE.** A clerk entered that a name was said in that yard and that a clerk may enter that a name was said and not what it was, and that the name is the name of a person who is one of the four hundred and eleven, and that **A NAME SAID OUT LOAD IN A YARD A STRANGER CAN WALK INTO IS NOT A CONSENT THE PERSON IT BELONGS TO GAVE, AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED, AND A NAME IS NOT A FIGURE, AND A NAME IN ONE PERSON'S HEAD IS NOT A RECORD AND IS NOT A COLUMN AND IS NOT A LINE ON A BOARD, AND EVERY PERSON IN THAT YARD HEARD TWO WORDS AND NONE OF THEM WAS WRITTEN DOWN BY ANYBODY.** She said one thing after it, in two hundred and thirty-eight words: that she is not going to hold it, that she has carried two buckets up that hill for nineteen years and there is one of the eleven she carries to that she has never put one question to her about in any of those nineteen years, and that twice she did ask that woman something and both times it was about a bucket, and that the woman in that house is not one of the four hundred and eleven and is not on any sheet in this district, and that she knows what that name is worth and does not know what it is worth, and that a name in one head can be got out of that head by anything at all, and that she is not asking for anything this evening. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN WHO PUTS TABLES UP ASKED HER ONE QUESTION AND WAS REFUSED IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HIS OWN: IF THE TWO WORDS WENT ON THE END OF THE TABLE HE PUTS UP AGAINST A WALL ABOUT FOUR FEET HIGH ELEVEN MILES OFF, A STRANGER COULD WALK UP ELEVEN MILES OF ROAD AND READ THEM, AND HE SAID IN THAT YARD IN THE SIXTH MONTH THAT A SHEET OF PRINT OUT WHERE A PERSON WALKS PAST IT IS WORTH MORE THAN A SHEET IN A ROOM, AND HE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE MAN WHO TAKES BACK A THING HE SAID OUT LOUD IN A YARD. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A DOCUMENT IN A ROOM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE IS A DOCUMENT IN A ROOM AND THAT THE SAME DOCUMENT NAILED TO A TABLE ELEVEN MILES OFF IS THE SAME DOCUMENT AND A DIFFERENT SET OF EYES ON IT.** **The man the figure of twenty-one years is against was at the end of that trestle table when she said it and was the only man in that yard who knew what the two words were, and said out loud at about ten past five, in a hundred and seventy-four words, that he heard it, that he is not going to say the two words back to show that he heard them, that he has got nothing to add to it and no correction and no reason why she should not have done it, and that he had a bad thing in his own head about what it is going to cost her and had had since yesterday afternoon, and that she got there first, and that he is not going up that bank tonight to tell a woman she should not have said it and is not going to say it was a good thing to say. A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE DID NOT GO UP THE BANK THAT EVENING AND DID NOT ASK ANYBODY ELSE TO GO UP IT AND SENT NOBODY, AND THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING.** The man of about sixty-four was at the foot of the low wall for the whole of that and did not stand up and did not look round, and about nine people looked at him and then looked away.

## Chapter 0338 — Twenty-Six Days Past

**The twenty-seventh of the eighth month, twenty-six days past. The board at 246, the train at 562, the unentered days at 276, two hundred and thirty-seven from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and twenty-nine days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-six days, the table for the fifty-second morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-seventh days, the rival record eighty-seven days, a hundred and forty-ninth day since anybody wrote on a board. IT IS NOT A VICTORY. THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS STILL FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN, THE SHEET IS STILL ON THE GATEPOST, AND THE SECOND LINE OF THE SHEET OF FIVE LINES OF PRINT IS STILL UNANSWERED.** A clerk entered that the second line names the second book four hundred yards from that gate and says the figure is to be completed in it, and that it carries the word **COMPLETED BY** over a ruled line with nothing on it, and that a document that names a job that does not exist has found nobody, and that the one person who may write in that book has not been asked anything about that line and the record about the not asking says not asked. **The man of about thirty-four who digs loam said out loud at about eleven, in two hundred and nineteen words, THAT THE SECOND OF THE TWO TERMS HOLDS IN THIS DISTRICT AND CANNOT HOLD ANYWHERE ELSE AND THAT BOTH OF THOSE ARE THE SAME FACT, THAT IT HOLDS HERE BECAUSE IT IS NAILED UP IN A YARD A STRANGER CAN WALK INTO AND THERE ARE ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE WHO HAVE READ IT OFF THAT TABLE WITH THEIR OWN EYES IN THE LAST TEN DAYS, THAT IT CANNOT HOLD ANYWHERE ELSE BECAUSE NOBODY OUTSIDE THIS BASIN HAS HEARD OF IT AND THERE IS NOTHING ON IT BUT TWO LINES OF TYPE AND A STONE ABOUT THE SIZE OF A FIST HOLDING THE CORNER DOWN, AND THAT THEY ARE NOT ALL RIGHT, AND THAT WE HAVE GOT ONE LINE OF TYPE AND ABOUT NINETEEN READERS AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE PROTECTION. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A TERM THAT HOLDS FOR THIS DISTRICT AND CANNOT HOLD FOR ANYBODY ELSE IS ENTERED AS HOLDING AND AS NOT HOLDING AND THE TWO ENTRIES ARE OF ONE TERM AND ARE NOT TWO TERMS, AND THAT THE STANDING OF IT IS NOT A SUCCESS.** **HIS RIGHT HAND CLOSED ABOUT A STONE ABOUT THE SIZE OF A FIST FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE THE TENTH OF THIS MONTH, AND HE CARRIED IT ABOUT FOUR INCHES AND SET IT DOWN ON THE OTHER CORNER, WITH A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS STANDING ABOUT NINE FEET OFF HIM WHO DID NOT OFFER TO DO IT AND DID NOT SAY ANYTHING ABOUT IT, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A HAND IS A FIGURE ABOUT A HAND AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A DOCUMENT, AND THAT THE ONE BOOK HE WOULD WANT TO WRITE IN IS THE SECOND OF THE TWO FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE AND THAT A HAND THAT CAN HOLD A STONE IS NOT A REASON TO GO UP THAT BANK.** He said out loud at about one, in two hundred and twelve words: that he wants the twenty-second of this month put down in one line because nobody is going to be able to work out later what it cost, that he left this district at about eight in the evening on the twenty-first and walked eleven miles up that road to the wall about four feet high where a table a man cannot set up has gone up fifty-two mornings running, that he sat at it until the middle of the night, that he walked the eleven miles back in the dark and was in a bed at about two and slept until about four, that his right hand has not closed since the tenth of this month, **THAT HE DID NOT HEAR THE BELL, THAT A SOUND THAT GOES ELEVEN MILES DID NOT WAKE A MAN FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OFF IT, THAT HE WAS THE MAN IN THIS DISTRICT WHO WAS NOT IN IT, AND THAT IT IS NOT A LESSON AND HE IS NOT GOING TO STAND IN A YARD AND TURN IT INTO ONE.** A clerk entered that the eleven miles that man walked and the eleven miles a man of about thirty-four kept a road brought back in the seventh month are the same length and are two figures and are not added together, and that nobody put either of them there. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN, AND THAT A NAME WAS SAID OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF THIS MONTH, AND THAT THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN HAS NOT MOVED AND IS NOT STRUCK, AND THAT THE REASON IS UNDERNEATH THIS ENTRY AND IS THAT A NAME IS NOT A FIGURE AND A NAME SAID OUT LOAD IS A CONSENT THE PERSON IT BELONGS TO DID NOT GIVE.** The eight lines of the roll were read out once in that yard at about half past five and read back from the top, and the list of about nineteen names read out on the twenty-third was not read out again and has not been read out again.

## Chapter 0339 — Nothing in His Hands

**The twenty-eighth of the eighth month, twenty-seven days past. The board at 247, the train at 563, the unentered days at 277, two hundred and thirty-eight from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and thirty days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-seven days, the table for the fifty-third morning, the six households a hundred and twenty-eighth days, the rival record eighty-eight days, a hundred and fiftieth day since anybody wrote on a board. THERE WAS NOTHING IN THE HANDS OF THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR THIS MORNING, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD SAID ONE WORD ABOUT THE HANDS.** He filled two buckets at the trough outside that wall at about eleven and carried them up the two goes of the bank to the building with two doors and put them down against the end wall, and about nine feet of brick with a bench and a stool in it took them without a word. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO CARRIES A MESSAGE FROM ONE PLACE TO ANOTHER ON HIS OWN TWO FEET IS NOT A KEEPER OF IT AND DOES NOT BECOME ONE, AND THAT A MAN WHOSE TRADE IS OVER IS NOT A FAILURE AND IS NOT A TRAGIC FIGURE AND IS NOT REDEEMED, AND THAT A MAN WHO CARRIES TWO BUCKETS OF WATER UP A BANK ON THE MORNING AFTER HE HAS SAID A NAME OUT LOUD IN A YARD IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A NAME AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A TRADE, AND THAT NOBODY IN THAT YARD SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT AND THAT THAT IS ENTERED AND IS NOT A FINDING AND IS NOT A REFUSAL AND IS NOT A REDEMPTION.** He said out loud at about half past twelve, in two hundred and fourteen words, that he has got nothing to carry and he is saying it out loud because if he does not somebody in that yard will spend the next month being careful at him about it, that he carried one thing for eighteen years and said it in a room three days ago, which was the twenty-fifth, and a woman of fifty-eight said it in that yard on the twenty-sixth, and that he is not a man who has lost a thing and is not a man who has been let go of anything, and that he is a man who has arrived and is going to be here, and that he is not asking any of them for a job or a wall or a line in a book, and that if one of them wants to give him a thing to do then ask him, because he will say yes to about half of it and no to the rest and will tell them which before he does it. **A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO IS STILL IN THIS DISTRICT THIRTY-EIGHT DAYS AFTER HE CAME INTO THAT GATE HAS NOT BEEN ASKED TO STAY AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED TO GO, AND THAT A STAY IS COUNTED WITHOUT THE DAY OF ARRIVAL AND THAT A MAN STAYING IS NOT A POST AND IS NOT A ROLE AND IS NOT A BEARER OF THAT WALL.** The man of about thirty-four who keeps a road was at the foot of that low wall all morning and nobody asked him one question about eleven miles. The woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale was at the bank with about nine sacks weighed, **and the four houses that stand empty and belong to her are not going to be offered again before the first day of the season after this one, on her own statement, and nobody has counted the people who have left them.** The man the figure of twenty-one years is against stood about nine feet off the end of that table from about ten until about four and did not go down to the low wall once, and a clerk entered that a man standing nine feet off a man for six hours has not said anything to him and is not standing in his place. **HE SAID OUT LOUD AT ABOUT FOUR, IN TWO HUNDRED AND THIRTY WORDS, THAT ON THE FIFTEENTH OF THIS MONTH HE WORKED OUT IN A ROOM THAT THE SOUND OF THAT BELL WENT OVER THE GROUND THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS ABOUT, AND THAT EVERY NAME IN A RECORD OF A BELL IS THE NAME OF SOMEBODY WHO DID NOT COME TO A DOOR, AND THAT SAYING ONE OF THOSE NAMES OUT LOUD HANDS IT TO A BODY FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF THAT IS ALREADY HOLDING A COUNT OF ANSWERERS, AND THAT HE HAS HAD NINE DAYS WITH THAT AND HAS NOT GOT A PRICE FOR IT AND IS NOT GOING TO PRETEND THAT NOT HAVING ONE IS A KIND OF KNOWING, AND THAT A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR TOLD HIM IN THE SIXTH MONTH THAT A SUBTRACTION IS ARITHMETIC AND NOT A FINDING AND THAT NOBODY IN THIS BASIN HAS EVER HAD A WAY OF PRICING A DIFFERENCE, AND THAT HE IS GOING TO LEAVE IT AS A THING THIS DISTRICT HAS NO WAY TO PRICE, IN PUBLIC, TODAY, SO THAT IT IS A THING AND NOT A SILENCE. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A NUMBER MADE BY KNOCKING, A NUMBER MADE BY WALKING AND A NUMBER MADE BY LISTENING ARE THREE NUMBERS AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER, AND THAT A NAME SAID OUT LOUD IN A YARD IS NOT ANY OF THE THREE AND IS NOT A FOURTH OF THEM AND IS NOT ADDED TO THEM, AND THAT A FIGURE THIS DISTRICT HAS NO WAY TO PRICE IS NOT A MYSTERY LEFT DANGLING AND IS NOT A PROMISE ABOUT A LATER DAY.**

## Chapter 0340 — One Line in Her Own Hand

**The twenty-ninth of the eighth month, twenty-eight days past, AND THE LAST DAY OF VOLUME 07. A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS ENTERED THAT THE LENGTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH IS NOT STATED IN THIS DISTRICT AND WAS NOT USED THIS MORNING AND NO FIGURE IN THIS DISTRICT HAS USED ONE, AND THAT A LADDER THAT STOOD AT NINETEEN ON THE TWENTIETH OF THIS MONTH HAS STOOD AT TWENTY-EIGHT AND IS A FIGURE ABOUT A LADDER. The board at 248, the train at 564, the unentered days at 278, two hundred and thirty-nine from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and thirty-one days back, the pool a hundred and fifty-eight days, the table for the fifty-fourth morning, the chair sat in once on the tenth of this month, the six households a hundred and thirtieth days, the rival record eighty-nine days and unanswered, a hundred and fifty-first day since anybody wrote on a board, and the man of about sixty-four at the foot of the low wall for the fortieth night, thirty-nine days in this district, and there is nothing in his hands.** A clerk entered at about two in the afternoon **THAT THE SECOND OF THE TWO BOOKS FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE HAS ONE LINE IN IT, THAT THE LINE IS IN THE HAND OF THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THAT BOOK, THAT SHE WROTE IT ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF THIS MONTH AND NOBODY ASKED HER TO WRITE IT AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED, THAT SHE DID NOT WRITE IT IN THE FIRST OF THE TWO BOOKS AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED ABOUT THE FIRST OF THE TWO BOOKS, THAT THE SECOND BOOK HAS BEEN OPEN SINCE THE SIXTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND HAS HAD NOTHING ENTERED IN IT UNTIL THAT AFTERNOON, THAT A LINE IN A BOOK IS A DOCUMENT AND IS NOT A DOCUMENT NOBODY OWNS AND IS NOT A FOURTH OF THE THREE THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE AND IS NOT A SEVENTH OF THE SIX INSTRUMENTS, AND THAT A LINE WITH A WORD IN IT THAT NAMES NOBODY IS NOT A LINE WITH A NAME ON IT.** The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud at about half past two, in two hundred and twenty-five words, that he heard it in that yard from a clerk who has been in it all morning, and that he is not going up that bank today, and that he is not going to ask the man who writes at that records office whether it is true, because if he asks then he has made it a thing he wanted to know and it is not a thing he wanted to know, it is a thing that happened to a woman in a room on her own two feet, and that there is one line in the second of those two books in this district for the first time in nine years and it is in her hand and nobody asked her for it, and that he is not going to be the man who reads that one out, and that she said in this basin on the twelfth of the seventh month that there was going to be one, and he has not asked her about it once since, and there it is. **The man the figure of twenty-one years is against was about nine feet off the end of that trestle table from about ten until about half past four and DID NOT GO UP THE BANK AND DID NOT STAND ON THE STEP OF TWO STONES AND DID NOT KNOCK, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT HE WAS TOLD NOTHING AND ASKED NOTHING AND SENT NOBODY AND THAT HE ASKED FOR NOTHING.** He said out loud at about half past four, in two hundred and twenty words, that he is not going to talk about that line and would be obliged if nobody in that yard talked about it to him, that that is not a favour to her and is not a favour to him, and then he listed what he has got and what he has not got: a roll with a column over it and nothing in the column, a pair of terms nailed up outside a building a reed man calls a good shed, a list of about nineteen names said out loud on the twenty-third, a name said in a yard on the twenty-sixth that is one of the four hundred and eleven, and one line in the second of those two books that somebody else wrote this afternoon; and that he has not got a way of pricing any of it, has not got a removal that did not happen explained, has not got a ninth charter's line or a third column of that covenant or the ninth place on that page, and has not got the two past pullings worked out and is not going to have them worked out by anybody in that yard this evening. **THE OLD CHARTER IS NOT CORRECTED AND NOT REPLACED AND NOT SUPERSEDED AND NOT TAKEN OUT AND ITS FIFTH LINE IS STILL WRONG ON ITS FACE, AND THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN IS NOT STRUCK. THE NINTH OF THE NINE PRINTED NIGHTS IS A HUNDRED AND THIRTY-ONE DAYS BACK AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT CAN SAY WHETHER THAT NIGHT HAPPENED, AND A PLANK DOES NOT KEEP NIGHTS.** The woman of fifty-eight went up that bank at about half past three with an empty bucket and came down it at about five with a full one and stopped at the foot of it for about nine minutes with nobody there, and a clerk entered that she was not asked about the line and that she was not asked about anything else. **The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing put his left hand flat on the outside wall of that building with two doors for about nine minutes and the wall was warm, and he took his hand off and put it in his coat and the wall stayed warm.** The couple did not settle and did not speak about it.

## Chapters 0331 to 0340 — the review-fix note on the summaries above, and the four figures that moved

**THE TEN SUMMARIES ABOVE WERE WRITTEN AGAINST THE TEXT AS IT FIRST STOOD AND A REVIEW FOUND NINE THINGS IN THAT TEXT, ALL NINE NOW REPAIRED. EVERY PLOT BEAT, DOCUMENT, FIGURE THAT DID NOT MOVE, SCENE, SPEECH AND DIALOGUE LINE IN ALL TEN CHAPTERS IS UNCHANGED, AND EVERY FIGURE IN ALL TEN SUMMARIES ABOVE STILL REPRODUCES AGAINST THE CHAPTERS EXCEPT THE FOUR BELOW. A WRITER WHO PICKS UP THESE SUMMARIES SHOULD READ THE CHAPTERS AND NOT THIS FILE FOR THE PROSE, AND SHOULD READ `state/volume-07-batch-0004-summary.md` SECTION 10 FOR WHAT MOVED AND `outline/batches/volume-07-batch-0004.md` SECTION 11 FOR THE NINE FINDINGS.**

**THE FOUR FIGURES THAT MOVED, AND BOTH SETS:**

- **THE NIGHTS ON THE LOW WALL. The summaries above print, at Chapter 331, that he has slept on that low wall on thirty of the thirty-one nights of his run, and at Chapter 340, thirty-nine of the forty, and every chapter between them the same shape. A stay is counted without the day he arrived on, so a thirty-day stay has thirty nights behind it. HE NOW HAS SLEPT ON THIRTY OF THIRTY AND THIRTY-ONE OF THIRTY-ONE AT CHAPTERS 331 AND 332, AND ON ONE FEWER OF THE NIGHTS THAT HAVE BEGUN FROM CHAPTER 333 ONWARD — THIRTY-ONE OF THIRTY-TWO, THIRTY-TWO OF THIRTY-THREE, THIRTY-THREE OF THIRTY-FOUR, THIRTY-FOUR OF THIRTY-FIVE, THIRTY-FIVE OF THIRTY-SIX, THIRTY-SIX OF THIRTY-SEVEN, THIRTY-SEVEN OF THIRTY-EIGHT, THIRTY-EIGHT OF THIRTY-NINE. THE NIGHT HE DID NOT SLEEP ON IS THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FIRST, WHEN HE STOOD IN THE SOUND. THE FIGURE HE FOLDS HIS COAT FOR IS THE NIGHT THAT HAS NOT HAPPENED YET AND IS THE STAY PLUS ONE, AND IT WAS ALWAYS RIGHT.**
- **THE FIGURES SPEECH. The summaries above print, in effect, that each day's man of about nineteen counted the same eight derived figures in the same order. HE DOES NOT. EACH DAY'S SPEECH NOW CARRIES THREE OR FOUR OF THEM IN AN ORDER OF ITS OWN, AND TWO OF THEM ARE ON THE BLOCK ONLY TWICE RATHER THAN ONCE EVERY DAY: *FOUR MONTHS BACK* AND THE INTERVAL TO THE NINTH OF THE NINE PRINTED NIGHTS. THE FIGURES THEMSELVES DID NOT MOVE AND EVERY ONE OF THEM STILL RE-DERIVES.**
- **THE FOOTER REFRAIN. The summaries above and the block record both print that all ten footers close on the same fourteen words. NONE OF THEM DOES. THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS STILL FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN ON ALL TEN DAYS AND IS STILL IN THE BODY OF ALL TEN — CHAPTER 332'S BODY INCLUDED, WHICH IT WAS NOT BEFORE — AND THE FOOTERS CLOSE IN NINE DIFFERENT SENTENCES.**
- **THE COUNTED CLAIM AT CHAPTER 331 IS TWO HUNDRED AND ELEVEN AND WAS TWO HUNDRED AND NINE, because *I held that rope the first time in June* became *in the sixth month*. The other nineteen claims in the block are unchanged and all twenty reproduce.**

**AND TWO CHAPTERS GAIN SCENE WITHOUT LOSING ANYTHING: Chapter 332, where the bell now goes in about five hundred words of night — the frame moving an inch, the rope going from hanging to hard, one word said out loud by somebody who will not say what it was, the man who keeps a road saying twice where the edge of the sound stopped, the man who digs loam sitting down on the turf and getting up off it on his own, the man of fifty-six down into a field on his forearms, and the man of about sixty-four with his hand on the left side of his head at about the fourth minute and not taking it off — and Chapter 336, which gained the morning of that ear, and Chapter 339, which gained ninety words in which the woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale asks about the weather and is not asked anything. Chapter 332 ALSO GAINED THE DAY-LEDGER THE OTHER NINE CHAPTERS CARRY AND IT DID NOT HAVE. NO DATE MOVED, NO COUNT THAT DID NOT MOVE CHANGED, NO THREAD CLOSED, AND THE NAME IS STILL NOT ON THIS PAGE.**

## Chapter 0341 — Thirty

**The thirtieth of the eighth month, twenty-nine days past, and THE LENGTH OF THAT MONTH IS ENTERED ON THE PAGE FOR THE FIRST TIME IN A HUNDRED AND FORTY CHAPTERS. The board at 249, the train at 565, the unentered days at 279, two hundred and forty days from the second of January, the first day of the eighth month twenty-nine days past. A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS COUNTED THE MARKS ON THE BOARD OUTSIDE THAT ROOM FROM THE MARK FOR THE FIRST OF THE EIGHTH MONTH TO THIS MORNING'S MARK AND ENTERED THIRTY, NAMING BOTH DAYS ALOUD WHILE SHE COUNTED THEM, AND ENTERED THE NINE MONTH LENGTHS AND THAT THE SECOND IS THE ONLY MONTH ANY PERSON IN THIS DISTRICT HAS EVER SAID WAS SHORT, AND THAT A MONTH NOBODY HAS SAID WAS SHORT IS A MONTH NOBODY HAS CHECKED, AND THAT THIS IS THE FIRST TIME THIS DISTRICT HAS CHECKED ONE, AND THAT THE FIGURE ON THE BOARD AND THE COUNT OFF IT ARE ONE FIGURE AND THAT A FIGURE THAT REPRODUCES IS A FIGURE ABOUT A COUNT AND IS NOT A FINDING. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING READ THE GROUPS ALOUD OFF THE WOOD BECAUSE HIS LEFT HAND IS IN A CLOTH AND CANNOT FEEL THE BOARD, AND HE SAID OUT LOUD THAT YESTERDAY WAS THE LAST DAY OF A MONTH THIS DISTRICT HAD ANY WAY OF STANDING ON, THAT THE THIRTIETH OF IT IS THE FIRST DAY A FIGURE FOR THAT MONTH CAN BE USED ON, THAT A PAGE WRITTEN FOUR DAYS AGO SAYING A LENGTH IS NOT STATED IS NOT WRONG AND IS NOT TO BE TAKEN BACK, AND THAT HE HAS BEEN IN THIS BASIN SINCE HE WAS NINE AND HAS NEVER ONCE KNOWN HOW LONG ANY MONTH WAS. THE COST: THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT STOOD AT THE FOOT OF THE BOARD FOR THE LENGTH OF THE COUNT AND HER WATER GOT TO THE END OF THAT BANK TWO HOURS LATE, AND NOBODY ASKED HER WHETHER SHE MINDED AND THE RECORD SAYS NOT ASKED. THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST CALLED THE BID FOR THE EVENING OF THE FIRST OF THE NINTH AND ENTERED THE TERM AS A MONTH WITH NO REASON GIVEN YET, BECAUSE HE WAS GOING TO ANSWER THE REASON OUT LOUD THE NEXT EVENING AND SAID SO IN THAT YARD. A hundred and fifty-second day with no line on a board. The figure on the sheet at that gatepost is four hundred and eleven and is ninety days old as a figure about the figure.**

## Chapter 0342 — A Lot With Two Doors

**The first of the ninth month, thirty days past, and THE FIRST BID IN THIS DISTRICT WAS OPENED. The train at 566, the unentered days at 280, two hundred and forty-one days from the second of January. THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST SPENT THE MORNING WALKING THE LANE AND DESCRIBING THAT BUILDING ALOUD TWICE TO HIMSELF AND LOSING THE THREAD AT THE BUCKETS BOTH TIMES, AND THE MAN WHO MENDS FENCING LET HIM FINISH BOTH TIMES WITHOUT HELPING HIM. He asked that yard one question at about four, whether anybody had a figure for what that building was worth for a month, AND ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE DID NOT ANSWER HIM, and he said out loud that he was not going to invent one at ten past six to save himself the look of a man who has not got one. At about six, in daylight that had not gone yet, in front of about nineteen people, HE DESCRIBED THE LOT OUT LOAD: about nine feet of brick and no window, a plank door in the end wall, a nail in a hasp that has not moved since the eleventh of the sixth month, a curtain on a nail, a bench, a stool, two buckets of water, a table with four legs against the outside wall, and a ditch about three feet deep with about a foot of standing water in it. NO FIGURE WAS READ OFF IT. THE TERM WAS SET AT A MONTH AND THE REASON WAS ENTERED: A BELL RUNG WHEN THERE IS NOTHING TO ANSWER IT CANNOT BE RUNG AGAIN FOR A MONTH, SO A MONTH IS THE SHORTEST INTERVAL THIS DISTRICT HAS EVER HAD TO KEEP A THING TRUE ACROSS, AND NOBODY ASKED FOR A LONGER ONE AND A CLERK DID NOT PROPOSE ONE. NO OFFER STOOD AND THE BID DID NOT CLOSE. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud at about seven that a bid cannot close here for three reasons, and a clerk entered that a bid is not a market and nobody in that yard said the word out loud. THE COST: HIS RIGHT HAND CLOSED ON THE EDGE OF THAT TABLE AT ABOUT A QUARTER PAST SIX AND DID NOT OPEN AGAIN UNTIL ABOUT TEN TO SEVEN, AND ABOUT NINE PEOPLE SAW IT AND NONE OF THEM SAID ANYTHING.**

## Chapter 0343 — Two Figures About One Refusal

**The second of the ninth month, thirty-one days past, AND NOTHING WAS ON THE END OF THAT TABLE FOR THE SECOND DAY RUNNING. The unentered days at 281, two hundred and forty-two days from the second of January, the ninth of the nine printed nights a hundred and thirty-four days back, the pool a hundred and sixty-one days, the rival record ninety-two days. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM WAS ASKED AT ABOUT ELEVEN WHETHER HE WOULD HOLD THAT BUILDING FOR A MONTH IF THE BID CAME TO HIM, AND HE SAID NO IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HIS OWN: that is the only thing in this district a person can get out of the weather in, and if it goes to whoever says the highest thing in that yard tonight then on the next wet night there is one person inside it under a term and everybody else is in the rain, and he is not going to be one of the people who says a number out loud about that. A CLERK ENTERED THAT A REFUSAL AND A REASON ARE TWO FIGURES ABOUT ONE THING AND ARE TWO FIGURES, AND THAT A REFUSAL WITH A REASON IS NOT ONE OF THE EIGHT REFUSALS WITH NO REASON A CLERK OF A HOUSE HAS GIVEN, AND THAT THOSE EIGHT DID NOT MOVE AND ARE STILL EIGHT, AND THAT A MAN OR A WOMAN WHO KEEPS A HOUSE IS NOT A CLERK OF A HOUSE. THE COST: THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT PUT TWO BUCKETS INSIDE THAT BUILDING THAT MORNING, STOOD FOUR MINUTES WITH HER HAND ON THE DOOR FRAME WHILE A MAN REFUSED TO STAND IN IT, SAID NOTHING, AND WAS ASKED NOTHING. The man of fifty-six said out loud that a reason is a thing a person can be asked for again and that the eight with no reason did not move. The rival record is unanswered and a bid a district runs on its own building is not a reading of it. Nine refusals of eleven coppers a week, seven about the ninth holding and eight with no reason are three figures and are not added together.**

## Chapter 0344 — Five Months Back

**The third of the ninth month, thirty-two days past. The train at 568, the unentered days at 282, the table against that wall eleven miles off for its fifty-eighth morning. AT ABOUT HALF PAST TEN THE MAN OF ABOUT NINETEEN, WHO COUNTS WHAT GETS SAID OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD AND IS NOBODY'S CLERK, SAID OUT LOUD THAT FROM THAT MORNING THE THIRD MONTH IS FIVE MONTHS BACK AND THAT THE PAGE HAS SAID FOUR SINCE THE THIRTY-FIRST OF THE SEVENTH AND THREE BEFORE THAT, AND THAT THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX SAID THE LAST PART OF IT IN THAT YARD ON THE THIRTY-FIRST OF THE SEVENTH, AND THAT HE HAS NOT ASKED HIM WHETHER HE AGREES AND IS NOT GOING TO, AND THAT A CLERK IS GOING TO WRITE FIVE AND IS NOT GOING BACK TO THE OLD PAGES, AND THAT HE SAID IT OUT LOUD IN A YARD AND NOT IN A ROOM. A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS WROTE IT IN ONE HAND ON ONE LINE WITH *LAST MONTH* AND *THE MONTH BEFORE LAST* IN A COLUMN OF THEIR OWN, AND ENTERED THAT A CORRECTION THAT GOES BACK AND EDITS THE THING IT CORRECTS CANNOT AFTERWARDS BE CHECKED, AND THAT A RELATIVE MONTH IS A FIGURE ABOUT WORDS AND NOT ABOUT A DATE, AND THAT THIS DISTRICT HAS TURNED THAT WORD FOUR TIMES SINCE THE FIRST OF THE SIXTH AND TWO OF THE FOUR HAPPENED IN THE NIGHT. THE COST: THE MAN THE FIGURE OF TWENTY-ONE YEARS IS AGAINST WROTE THE THREE AND FOUR ON THOSE PAGES AND HELD THE NEW ONE FLAT AGAINST THE WIND WITH A RIGHT HAND THAT COULD NOT CLOSE ON THE EDGE OF IT AT ALL, AND SAID OUT LOAD THAT HE IS NOT GOING BACK AND THAT BOTH FIGURES WERE WRONG AND ONE IS NEARER, AND THAT A CORRECTION IS NOT CONSENT. Nobody may go back and alter an old ledger page. The pool is five months back and a hundred and sixty-two days. ## Chapter 0345 — Sixty-One Days

**The fourth of the ninth month, thirty-three days past. The unentered days at 283, two hundred and forty-four days from the second of January, the pool a hundred and sixty-three days. HE SAID OUT LOUD AT ABOUT HALF PAST TEN THAT HE IS NOT AFRAID OF THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN AND THAT WHAT HE IS AFRAID OF IS SIXTY-ONE DAYS, WHICH IS THE INTERVAL BETWEEN THE DAY THAT SHEET WAS FOUND ON THE FIRST OF THE SIXTH AND THE DAY IT NAMES, WHICH IS THE FIRST OF THE EIGHTH, AND THAT A FIGURE ABOUT HOW OFTEN A BODY FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF PRINTS IS A FIGURE ABOUT A HABIT AND NOT ABOUT THE BODY, AND THAT HE IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THIS DISTRICT WHO KNOWS WHAT A COUNT OF ANSWERERS IS AND WHAT A LIST OF HEARERS IS AND WHAT THE DIFFERENCE COSTS, AND THAT HE IS THE ONLY PERSON HERE WHO CANNOT LEARN IT BACK FROM ANYBODY. AND THEN HE SAID, IN THE SAME YARD, THAT HE HAS STOOD THERE SINCE THE FIRST OF THE SIXTH AND HAS NEVER GIVEN ANYBODY ONE WORD TO CALL HIM BY, AND THAT NOT ONE OF THE ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE STANDING THERE COULD TELL A STRANGER WHAT TO CALL HIM, AND THAT HE IS NOT STARTING TONIGHT, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO SAY IT A SECOND TIME. NOBODY ASKED HIM FOR ONE AND THE RECORD SAYS NOT ASKED AND NO COLUMN WAS RULED AND A CLERK MAY ENTER THAT A MAN SAID HE HAS NOT GOT A WORD FOR ANYBODY TO CALL HIM BY AND MAY NOT ENTER WHAT IT IS. THE COST: A WIND CAME UP THE LANE AND THE WEST LEG OF THAT TABLE LIFTED AND WOULD NOT STAY DOWN UNDER HIS HAND, AND HIS RIGHT HAND COULD NOT GET ROUND THE LEG AN INCH, AND A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS CAME AND PUT HER OWN HAND ON THE OTHER SIDE AND HELD IT WHILE ABOUT NINE PEOPLE WEDGED IT WITH THEIR BOOTS, AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID ONE WORD TO THE OTHER. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing said out loud that he is not going to be one of the men in that yard who starts finding jobs for a man of sixty-four because a bell went round twice.**

THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING SPENT SEVEN HOURS BENDING WIRE AT THE END OF THAT TABLE WITH ONE HAND AND NOBODY ASKED HIM WHAT IT WAS FOR. THE CONVENOR SAID OUT LOUD THAT THIS YARD HAS HAD A BID IN IT FOR FOUR DAYS AND ON FIVE OF SIX THERE HAS BEEN NOTHING ON THE TABLE, AND THAT A DISTRICT THAT BUILDS AN INSTRUMENT AND THEN STANDS ABOUT IN IT ON ONE DAY OUT OF TEN IS NOT A DISTRICT THAT HAS BUILT NOTHING. THE COST: AT ABOUT HALF PAST FIVE THE MAN WHO MENDS FENCING COULD NOT GET HIS FINGERS STRAIGHT TO UNDO HIS OWN BOOT LACE, AND THE MAN WHO KEEPS A ROAD DID IT FOR HIM IN ABOUT NINE SECONDS, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A MAN WHO UNDOES ANOTHER MAN'S BOOT LACE HAS NOT TAKEN ON A POST. AT ABOUT TWO THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES A CART ASKED ONE QUESTION OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE: WHAT HAPPENS IF NOBODY EVER ANSWERS IT. NOBODY ANSWERED HIM. THE COST: THE CART WAS DUE AT THE FAR END OF THAT ROAD AT ABOUT THREE AND WENT OUT OF THAT LANE AT ABOUT TEN PAST FOUR INSTEAD, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT AN HOUR OF A CARTING DAY IS A FIGURE ABOUT AN HOUR AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT A QUESTION NOBODY ANSWERED, AND THAT NOBODY PAID FOR THAT HOUR AND THE MAN WHO LOST IT DID NOT SAY HE HAD. The man of fifty-six said out loud that a thing nobody answers stops being a question and turns into a wall, and that he is not saying it ought to be answered. The pair of terms were read out loud and the second of them twice, and the fourth line of the offer was not read out loud and the refusals to read are nine and there is still no date in any ledger for the next reading. NOBODY PROPOSED A READING.**
